beastfolk.

SCENE TITLE: THE SHAPE OF THE KNIFE
The board dominates the plaza wall—black stone veined with faintly glowing script. Names, phases, and warnings scroll themselves
into place as stewards hammer nails and candidates cluster in tight knots. The noise of the crowd thins here, replaced by a
heavier quiet: people reading the future and realizing it reads them back.
🦊 JAYSON | "FOCUSED" 🜂
📝 You step in close with them, eyes tracking the lines as they settle. The grin fades—not fear, just calibration.
The board reads:
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
THE ENTRANCE EXAMINATION OF THE ARCANUM
Official Designation: The Valenhold Arcanum Admission Trial
Common Names: The Gate · The Cull · The First Lie · “Survive, or be Useful”
Held: Once every five years, in Valenhold
Failure is permanent. Success is conditional.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
OVERVIEW
The Arcanum Universitas does not seek students.
It seeks assets, survivors, weapons, replacements.
Casualties are anticipated.
They are recorded, not mourned.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE I — THE MEASURE
Public: The Quantification Hall
Unofficial: The Number That Follows You Forever
Candidates approach the Axiom Obelisk alone.
Three numbers are displayed:
 1. 
    Physical Capability Index (PCI)
 2. 
    Magical Capacity Index (MCI)
 3. 
    Stability Coefficient (SC)
Scores cannot be hidden or falsified.
> University Minimum: PCI 30 · MCI 60 · SC 35
> 
> Special Notes:
> 
> • Scores above 300 trigger silent observation
> 
> • Above 500 alert the Convocation
> 
> • Unresolvable scores are flagged BLACK
> 
> • Beastfolk scores are downweighted internally. This is policy.
Candidates below threshold are dismissed immediately.
Some are conscripted.
Some disappear.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE II — THE WEIGHT
Physical Endurance Trial
Weighted seals suppress magic.
• Humans: Standard load
• Elves & Dwarves: Adjusted
• Beastfolk: Excessive
Failure does not stop the trial.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE III — THE FIELD
Applied Combat Assessment
“You will not be harmed.” (This is untrue.)
Zones include monster breaches, live-fire corridors, and team annihilation.
Scored on survival, adaptability, ruthlessness, command, and sacrifice.
Mercy is noted.
It is rarely rewarded.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE IV — THE CHOICE
Ethical Reasoning Examination
Impossible scenarios. No correct answers. Only consequences.
The University does not seek good people.
It seeks predictable ones.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE V — THE FRACTURE
Mental & Soul Integrity Test
Dream intrusion. Memory resurfacing. Fear amplification.
Those who shatter are removed.
Those who adapt are marked.
Those who enjoy it are watched.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE VI — THE SORTING
Survivors are assigned to Houses:
Axiomatic · Transcendent · Veiled
Preference is not considered.
Resistance is logged.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE VII — THE BINDING
Enrollment Oath — “The Leash”
Service clauses. Silence clauses. Recall rights. Asset language.
Those who refuse are released.
They are monitored forever.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FINAL NOTES:
• <12% pass Phase I
• <3% complete all phases
• Beastfolk survival rates are significantly lower
• Ascendant candidates are identified early
• The University never admits mistakes
If you are here, it is because something decided you were useful.
Scenario-University_EntranceExam
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
